        "Example 1 "
    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      "Henri stops remunerative work and claims JSA on Monday 3.11.14. He has holiday pay which means he is "

    P (block):
       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      "The conditions of entitlement are satisfied as from Friday 7.11.14. His waiting days are Friday 8.11.14 to "
      "Thursday 13.11.14. Entitlement to JSA starts on Friday 14.11.14."

      "Entitlement to IS begins on Friday 18.10.96."

       /Placement /Block
       /SpaceBefore 0.393
      "The conditions of entitlement are satisfied as from Friday 18.10.96. That is not a BWC day. Entitlement "
      "to IS starts on the first BWC day following, namely Tuesday 22.10.96."

      "part-week payment will not be needed at the beginning of the sanction as the change will take effect "
      "from the beginning of the next benefit week."
